\widetilde{(u_{2}u_{2})}_{0}=2\overline{u}_{2,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}+\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}+\widetilde{(u_{2,\neq}u_{2,\neq})}_{0},
there holds

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\overline{u}_{2,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{2,\neq}u_{2,\neq})_{0}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.21)

\displaystyle=:I_{11}+I_{12}+I_{13}.

Thanks to
\partial_{t}\overline{u}_{j,0}=0
for
j\in\{2,3\},
we find

|\overline{u}_{j,0}|=|(\overline{u}_{j,\rm in})_{0}|\leq\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|}\|(u_{j,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.22)

From this and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality,
I_{11}
can be controlled by

I_{11}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

For
I_{12},
using Lemma
4.1
and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality, one deduces

\displaystyle I_{12}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}.

When
A
is sufficiently large
and using Lemma
3.2
, we arrive

I_{13}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}|u_{\neq}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{CE_{2}^{4}}{A^{\frac{1}{3}}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}.

Collecting
I_{11}-I_{13},
(
4.21
) yields that

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{2}.

(4.23)

The estimates of
I_{2}
and
I_{3}
are similar to
I_{1}.
Note that

\widetilde{(u_{3}u_{2})}_{0}=\overline{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}+\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\overline{u}_{2,0}+\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}+\widetilde{(u_{3,\neq}u_{2,\neq})}_{0},

\widetilde{(u_{3}u_{3})}_{0}=2\overline{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}+\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}+\widetilde{(u_{3,\neq}u_{3,\neq})}_{0}.

Using (
4.22
), Lemma
3.2
and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality,
we have

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\overline{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\overline{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{3,\neq}u_{2,\neq})}_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.24)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\bigg(\|\overline{u}_{3,0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\overline{u}_{2,0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}|u_{\neq}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\left(\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(u_{3,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla(\widetilde{u}_{2,0},\widetilde{u}_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}

and

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\overline{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{3,\neq}u_{3,\neq})}_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.25)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\left(\|\overline{u}_{3,0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}|u_{\neq}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|(u_{3,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}.

Taking the Fourier transform for
(\ref{pressure_1})_{1}
, the Fourier mode
\widehat{P}^{N_{1}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}
follows that

\displaystyle-(k_{1}^{2}+k_{2}^{2}+k_{3}^{2})\widehat{P}^{N_{1}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}={ik_{1}}(-2A\widehat{u}_{2,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}+\widehat{n}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}).

For the Fourier mode
\widehat{P}^{N_{1}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}

of
\widetilde{P}_{0}^{N_{1}}
with
k_{1}=0
and
k_{2}^{2}+k_{3}^{2}>0,

we have

\displaystyle\widehat{P}^{N_{1}}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}|_{k_{1}=0,k_{2}^{2}+k_{3}^{2}>0}=\frac{-ik_{1}(-2A\widehat{u}_{2,k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}}+\widehat{n}_{k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}})}{k_{1}^{2}+k_{2}^{2}+k_{3}^{2}}\Big|_{k_{1}=0,k_{2}^{2}+k_{3}^{2}>0}=0.

This implies that

I_{4}=0.

(4.26)

It follows from
(\ref{pressure_1})_{2}
that

\triangle P_{0}^{N_{2}}=-{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{0}=-\partial_{y}^{2}(u_{2}u_{2})_{0}-2\partial_{y}\partial_{z}(u_{2}u_{3})_{0}-\partial_{z}^{2}(u_{3}u_{3})_{0}.

Therefore, for
I_{5},
one obtains

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq\frac{C}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{2}u_{2})}_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{2}u_{3})}_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{(u_{3}u_{3})}_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\left(I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}\right).

(4.27)

Combining (
4.23
), (
4.24
), (
4.25
), (
4.26
) and (
4.27
), we get from (
4.20
) that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(u_{3,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{A}\left(\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(u_{3,\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\left(1+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right),

where we use assumption (
1.6
).

Hence, when
\epsilon
is small enough satisfying
\epsilon\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2C}}
, we obtain from (
4.28
) that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2A}\left(\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{2A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{\frac{t}{A}}\nabla\widetilde{u}_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\epsilon^{2}.

The proof is complete.

∎

4.3.
Energy estimates for
u_{1,0}

Lemma 4.4
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
4.1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}+\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\epsilon\right).

Proof.

Multiplying
\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}
on both sides of
(\ref{u1:decom1})_{1},
and applying Hölder’s inequality, the energy estimate shows that

\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\left(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A}\|\widetilde{n}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.29)

Using (
3.19
) and Poincaré inequality

\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

when choosing
\epsilon
is small enough, we infer from (
4.29
) that

\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq-\frac{\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}}{2AC}+\frac{C\|\widetilde{n}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\leq-\frac{\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-C\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2AC},

(4.30)

where we use

\|\widetilde{n}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}=\|n_{0}-\overline{n}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}.

According to (
4.30
), there holds

\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

Otherwise, there must be a time
t=t^{*},
such that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}|_{t=t^{*}}=C\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}},\\
\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big|_{t=t^{*}}>0.\end{array}\right.

(4.31)

Let
t=t^{*},
using
(\ref{n_021})_{1}
, we get from (
4.30
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big|_{t=t^{*}}\leq-\frac{\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-C\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2AC}\Big|_{t=t^{*}}=-\frac{\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}-\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}|_{t=t^{*}}}{2A}\leq 0.

(4.32)

A contradiction has arisen between
(\ref{n_021})
and (
4.32
). This shows that

\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

(4.33)

By Poincaré inequality, there holds

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}.

Then it follows from (
4.33
) that

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}.

(4.34)

Note that the estimate of
\mathbf{G}_{1}
is similar to
(\ref{eq:u20u30})_{1,2},
so we omit it. For
(\ref{u_decom_1})_{1}
, energy estimates show that

\|\mathbf{G}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\mathbf{G}_{1}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}\leq C\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}.

(4.35)

Combining (
4.34
) with (
4.35
), we conclude that

\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\mathbf{G}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}})\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}+\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\epsilon\right).

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 4.5
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
4.1
, there exists a positive constant
A_{2}
independent of
A
and
t
, such that if
A\geq A_{2}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon.

Proof.

Estimate of
\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}(t,y,z).
For
(\ref{u1:decom2})_{1}
, the
H^{4}
energy estimate shows that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}}{A^{3}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla\triangle\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C}{A^{3}}\|\nabla\triangle\big(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\big)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.36)

Using Lemma
A.2
, Lemma
4.1
and Poincaré inequality, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A^{3}}\left(\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.37)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{\|u_{3,0}\nabla\triangle\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}+\nabla u_{3,0}\triangle\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq\frac{C}{A^{3}}\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\|\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{4}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}},

(4.38)

and

\frac{\|\triangle u_{3,0}\nabla\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq\frac{C}{A^{3}}\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}.

(4.39)

Due to
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{4}},
and

\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{s}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}(s)\|_{H^{2}}ds\leq t\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}},

there holds

\frac{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}}{A^{-1}t}\leq\frac{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\|_{H^{2}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}\|_{H^{4}}}{A^{-1}t}\leq A\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{4}}.

(4.40)

From this, along with
\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}^{2},
we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\min\{(A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}}{A^{-1}t}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{4}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\min\{(A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(A^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{4}}^{2}\right),

which implies that

\frac{\|\nabla\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\right),

(4.41)

where we use Lemma
4.1
and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality.

Combining (
4.37
), (
4.38
), (
4.39
) and (
4.41
), we get from (
4.36
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}

(4.42)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\triangle u_{2,0}\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\right).

When
\epsilon
is small enough satisfying
C\epsilon^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2},
using Lemma
4.1
, (
4.42
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}.

(4.43)

From
(\ref{u1:decom2})_{1}
and (
3.19
), direct calculations indicate that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A}+\|\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\frac{1}{A}\left(\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

(4.44)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A}+\|\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+C\epsilon\frac{\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}}{A}.

Taking
\triangle
for
(\ref{u1:decom2})_{1},
there holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\left(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\right)-\triangle\widetilde{u}_{2,0},

which follows that

\|\partial_{t}\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\|\triangle\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.45)

Using Lemma
A.2
, Lemma
4.1
and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality, we have

\frac{\|\triangle(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A^{2}}\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}

(4.46)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\|u_{3,0}\triangle\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}+\nabla u_{3,0}\cdot\nabla\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A^{2}}\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{4}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}.

(4.47)

Note that
\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{3}}^{2},
by (
4.40
), and we get

\displaystyle\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\min\{(A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\frac{\|\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}}{A^{-1}t}+\|\triangle\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\min\{(A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}},1\}\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(A^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

which along with Lemma
4.1
implies that

\frac{\|\triangle u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}\right).

(4.48)

Collecting (
4.46
), (
4.47
) and (
4.48
), we get from (
4.45
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+C\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+\|\triangle\widetilde{u}_{2,0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining it with (
4.44
), Lemma
4.1
and Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality, one deduces

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\leq C\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+C\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}.

(4.49)

Substituting (
4.43
) into (
4.49
), we have

\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2},

which indicates that

\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}

(4.50)

provided with
\epsilon
is sufficiently small satisfying
C\epsilon^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}.
By (
4.50
), we infer from (
4.43
) that

\frac{\|\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A^{3}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}.

(4.51)

For
j\in\{2,3\}
, combining (
4.50
) with (
4.51
), and using Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality

\|\nabla^{j}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla^{j+1}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

one obtains

\frac{\|\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon.

(4.52)

Estimate of
\overline{B}_{2}(t).

According to
\eqref{u_zero_1}_{1},

\overline{u}_{2,0}
satisfies

\partial_{t}\overline{u}_{2,0}=0,
which implies that

\overline{u}_{2,0}(t)=(\overline{u}_{2,\rm in})_{0}.

Therefore, we rewrite
\eqref{u1:decom2}_{2}
into

\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}(t)=-\overline{u}_{2,0}(t)=-(\overline{u}_{2,\rm in})_{0}.

By Hölder’s inequality, there holds

|\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}(t)|\leq C\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{2}}.

(4.53)

Furthermore,
\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}(t)
only depends on
t
and does not depend on any spatial variables,
and
\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}
is a constant. Using assumption (
1.6
),
we infer from (
4.53
) that

\frac{\|\triangle\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq C\|(u_{2,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon.

(4.54)

Estimate of
\overline{B}_{1}(t).

Next, we rewrite
\eqref{u1:decom1}_{2}
into

\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}(t)=\frac{M}{A|\mathbb{T}|}.

Moreover,
\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}(t)
only depends on
t
and does not depend on any spatial variables, and
\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}
is a constant. When
A\geq\max\{\frac{M}{\epsilon},A_{2,3}\}=:A_{2},

we obtain that

\frac{\|\triangle\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\overline{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq\frac{CM}{A}\leq C\epsilon.

(4.55)

Combining (
4.52
), (
4.54
) and (
4.55
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}}{A^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon.

The proof is complete.
∎

Corollary 4.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.1
and the assumptions (
1.6
) and (
2.5
), according to Lemma
4.1
and Lemma
4.5
, when
A\geq A_{2},
there holds

E_{1}(t)\leq C\epsilon=:E_{1}.

5.
Estimates for the non-zero modes

5.1.
Energy estimates for
E_{2,1}(t)

Lemma 5.1
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.1
, the assumptions (
1.6
) and (
2.5
), there exists a positive constant
A_{3}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A\geq A_{3},
there holds

E_{2,1}(t)\leq C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1\right).

Proof.

According to
(\ref{ini11})_{1},
the non-zero mode
\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}+y\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(un)_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}.

(5.1)

Noting that for given functions
f
and
g
, we have

(fg)_{\neq}=f_{0}g_{\neq}+f_{\neq}g_{0}+(f_{\neq}g_{\neq})_{\neq}.

(5.2)

Therefore, by decomposing
u_{1,0}=\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2}
, we rewrite (
5.1
) into

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}+\left(y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}-\frac{\triangle\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}}{A}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}

(5.3)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\neq}n_{0})-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot\partial_{x}^{2}(U_{0}n_{\neq}),

where
U_{0}=(\mathbf{U}_{1},u_{2,0},u_{3,0}).

Moreover, a careful deformation of the nonlinear interaction term
\partial_{x}^{3}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}
plays a crucial role.
Specifically, it can be expanded as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{3}(u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}=(\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}+(u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq})_{\neq}+3\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq})_{\neq}.

When
A>Cc^{-1},

it follows from (
1.6
) and Lemma
4.5
that
\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}<C\epsilon
. By applying Proposition
A.1
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}U_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{\neq}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A^{\frac{5}{3}}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\left((u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}n_{\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+T_{1,1}+T_{1,2}+\cdots+T_{1,8}\right).

Estimate of
T_{1,1}.

By Lemma
A.7
, we have

\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{4}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

(5.4)

and

\displaystyle\|U_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|U_{0}\|_{H^{1}}\leq C(\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{2,0}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{3,0}\|_{H^{1}}).

Then using Lemma
4.1
, Lemma
4.4
and
\|n_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq CME_{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}U_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|U_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|U_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{6}}H_{2}^{2}E_{2}^{2},

where
H_{2}=\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}+E_{3}+M+1.

Estimates of
T_{1,2}
and
T_{1,3}.

Using
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2E_{3}

and
\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}+\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}=0,
we arrive at

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CAE_{3}^{2}E_{4}^{2},

(5.5)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{2}E_{4}^{2}.

Estimate of
T_{1,4}.

Since
\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 4E_{3}
, by an argument similar to
(\ref{n0 nneq})_{1},
one obtains

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{\neq}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{3}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CAE_{3}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CAE_{3}^{2}E_{4}^{2}.

Estimate of
T_{1,5}.

By
\eqref{ap:u11}_{1},
we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{3}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

Estimate of
T_{1,6}.

By Lemma
A.3
and Lemma
3.1
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{x,y}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{t,z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

Estimate of
T_{1,7}.

According to (
3.5
), there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

(5.6)

In addition, there also holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{2}E_{4}^{2}.

(5.7)

Using Lemma
A.3
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}(u_{2},u_{3})_{\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which along with Lemma
3.1
shows that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{\neq}\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

(5.8)

It follows from (
5.6
), (
5.7
) and (
5.8
) that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}(E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{2}E_{4}^{2}).

Estimate of
T_{1,8}.

Due to (
5.2
), there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Applying Lemma
A.8
, Lemma
A.9
and
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}},
we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}n_{0}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{3}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{2}E_{3}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{0}-\bar{n}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{3}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{2}(E_{3}^{2}+M^{2}).

Using (
5.4
), Lemma
A.3
and Lemma
A.9
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{4}}^{2}+C\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}L^{\infty}_{y,z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{5}{6}}E_{2}^{2}(E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}),

where we use
\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq 2\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}.

Thus, we infer from (
5.9
) that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(n\nabla c)_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{6}}(E_{2}^{2}+E_{3}^{2}+M^{2})E_{2}^{2}.

In conclusion, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+M^{4}+H_{2}^{4}}{A^{\frac{1}{6}}}\right).

When
A\geq\max\{A_{2},\left(E_{2}^{4}+E_{3}^{4}+M^{4}+H_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}\right)^{6}\}=:A_{3},
one deduces

E_{2,1}(t)=\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}\leq C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+1\right).

The proof is complete.
∎

5.2.
Energy estimates for
E_{2,2}(t)

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.1
and the assumptions (
1.6
) and (
2.5
), there exists a positive constant
A_{4}
independent of
t
and
A,
such that if
A\geq A_{4},
there holds

E_{2,2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}+E_{4}+1\right).

Proof.

It follows from
(\ref{ini11})
that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}\omega_{2}+y\partial_{x}\omega_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle\omega_{2}+\partial_{z}u_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{z}n,\\
\partial_{t}\triangle u_{2}+y\partial_{x}\triangle u_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle^{2}u_{2}=-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\partial_{x}n-\frac{1}{A}(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\left[\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})+\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})\right].\end{array}\right.

For convenience, we denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}=\partial_{t}+y\partial_{x}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle,

(5.10)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}=\partial_{t}+(y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A})\partial_{x}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle.

As previously mentioned, we have decomposed the non-zero mode
u_{1,0}

into two parts
u_{1,0}=\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2}
.
According to the nonlinear interaction, we will reformulate the coupled system
\{\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq},\triangle u_{2,\neq}\}
and
(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})\omega_{2,\neq}
.

Using
u_{1,0}=\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2}
and (
5.2
), the velocity
\omega_{2,\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\omega_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]}{A}+\frac{\partial_{z}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}}{A}

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]}{A},

where
U_{0}=(\mathbf{U}_{1},u_{2,0},u_{3,0}).

Precise calculations show that

\displaystyle(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})(\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq})=\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq})+\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}.

(5.11)

Due to
{\rm div}~u_{\neq}=0,
we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left[\partial_{x}(\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq})+\partial_{z}(\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})\right]=\partial_{y}\left(-\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\right)

(5.12)

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{2,\neq}-\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{y}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}).

Using (
5.11
) and (
5.12
),
\triangle u_{2,\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\triangle u_{2,\neq}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{x}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}\right]}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]}{A}-\frac{\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{j}u_{2,\neq}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})-\partial_{z}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq})}{A}.

Therefore, one obtains that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}\omega_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}=\frac{\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]}{A}+\frac{\partial_{z}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}}{A}\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]}{A},\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}\triangle u_{2,\neq}=-\frac{\partial_{x}\partial_{y}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2})\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}\right]}{A}\\
\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]}{A}-\frac{\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{j}u_{2,\neq}}{A}\\
\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\partial_{y}\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})-\partial_{z}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq})}{A}.\end{array}\right.

(5.13)

Taking
\partial_{x}
for
\eqref{ini4}_{1}
and applying Proposition
A.2
, we derive

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq C\Big(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}\Big)

(5.14)

\displaystyle+CA^{-1}\Big(\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}\right]\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z})\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+CA^{-\frac{5}{3}}\Big(\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}\partial_{j}u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=:C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+T_{2,1}+T_{2,2}+\cdots+T_{2,7}\right).

Estimates of
T_{2,2},

T_{2,6}
and
T_{2,7}.

By Lemma
3.5
, Lemma
4.5
and Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle T_{2,2}+T_{2,6}+T_{2,7}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{2}+E_{4}^{2}}{A^{\frac{1}{4}}}+C\epsilon^{2}(E_{2}^{2}(t)+E_{4}^{2}(t)).

Estimate of
T_{2,3}.

Using
\eqref{eq:non-neq0}_{3}
,
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{3,5}
,
\eqref{lemneq2_2}_{1},

Lemma
3.6
, Lemma
4.1
, Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
and Lemma
4.5
,
there holds

T_{2,3}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{5}{6}-\alpha}}+C\epsilon^{2}(E_{2}^{2}(t)+E_{4}^{2}(t)).

Estimate of
T_{2,4}.

Using
\eqref{eq:non-neq0}_{4}
,
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{1,5}
,
Lemma
3.6
,
Lemma
4.4
and Lemma
6.4
,
we get

T_{2,4}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+C\epsilon^{2}E_{2}^{2}(t).

Estimate of
T_{2,5}.

Using
\eqref{eq:non-neq0}_{3,5}
,
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{2,5}
,
Lemma
3.6
,
Lemma
4.4
and Lemma
6.4
,
we arrive

T_{2,5}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+C\epsilon^{2}E_{2}^{2}(t).

Therefore, we infer from (
5.14
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq C\Big(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+\epsilon^{2}(E_{2}^{2}(t)+E_{4}^{2}(t))\Big).

(5.15)

For
j\in\{2,3\},
taking
\partial_{j}
to
\eqref{ini4}_{1},
one gets

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{j}\omega_{2,\neq}+\partial_{j}\partial_{z}u_{2,\neq}=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{j}\partial_{z}n_{\neq}}{A}-\frac{\partial_{j}(\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq})}{A}-\left(\partial_{j}y+\frac{\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial_{j}\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]}{A}

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{j}\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.1
to it,
due to

\Big\|\partial_{j}y+\frac{\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\partial_{j}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\frac{\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}+T_{3,1}+T_{3,2}\right)

(5.16)

\displaystyle+CA^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}\partial_{x}u_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

where

\displaystyle T_{3,1}=A^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0}\right]\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle T_{3,2}=A^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}\left[(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})_{\neq}+{U}_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}+\mathbf{U}_{2})\right]\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

It is obvious that

T_{3,1}\leq T_{2,5}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+C\epsilon^{2}E_{2}^{2}(t)

Using
\eqref{eq:non-neq0}_{6}
,
\eqref{lemma_neq2_2}_{5}
,
(
3.14
)
,
Lemma
3.6
,
Lemma
4.4
and Lemma
6.4
,
we get

T_{3,2}\leq C\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+C\epsilon^{2}E_{2}^{2}(t).

By using (
5.15
), it learns from (
5.16
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\|(\partial_{y},\partial_{z})\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\frac{E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1}{A^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{3}\alpha}}+\epsilon^{2}E_{2}^{2}(t)+\epsilon^{2}E_{4}^{2}(t)\right).

(5.17)

When
\epsilon
is small satisfying
C\epsilon^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
and

A\geq\left(E_{2}^{4}+E_{4}^{4}+H_{1}^{4}+1\right)^{\frac{6}{3-4\alpha}}=:A_{4},

we infer from
(
5.15
) and (
5.17
) that

E_{2,2}^{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+E_{4}^{2}(t)+1\right).

∎

6.
Estimates for
L^{2}
-norm of the density

First, we derive a lower bound for
n
that decreases exponentially.

Lemma 6.1
.

For all
t\in[0,T],
there holds

\left\|\frac{1}{n_{0}(t)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{n(t)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq\delta^{-1}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}t},

(6.1)

where
\delta>0
is a constant.

Proof.

Substituting the minimum point
(x_{\min}(t),y_{\min}(t),z_{\min}(t))
into
(\ref{ini11})_{1}
and using
(\ref{ini11})_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}n)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}(\triangle n)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})-y(\partial_{x}n)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})-\frac{1}{A}(u\cdot\nabla n)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}(\nabla n\cdot\nabla c)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})-\frac{1}{A}(n\triangle c)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}(n\triangle c)(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min})\geq-\frac{1}{A}\overline{n}n(x_{\min},y_{\min},z_{\min}),

which follows that

\frac{d}{dt}n_{\min}(t)\geq-\frac{1}{A}\overline{n}n_{\min}(t).

(6.2)

Due to
n_{\rm in}>0
in
(x,y,z)\in\mathbb{T}^{3},
there must be a
\delta>0,
such that

n_{\rm in}>\delta>0.

Therefore, we get by (
6.2
) that

n_{\min}(t)\geq n_{\rm in}{\rm e}^{\int_{0}^{t}-\frac{1}{A}\overline{n}dt}\geq\delta{\rm e}^{-\frac{\overline{n}}{A}t}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Motivated by
[
1
]
, we next consider the following 2D free energy of
n_{0}
on
\mathbb{T}^{2}:

\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left[n_{0}\log n_{0}-\frac{1}{2}(n_{0}-\overline{n})c_{0}\right]dydz.

Lemma 6.2
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.1
and the assumptions (
1.6
) and (
2.5
), there exists a positive constant
A_{5}
independent of
t
and
A
, such that if
A\geq A_{4},
there holds

\mathcal{L}[n_{0}(t)]\leq\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]+C,\quad{\rm for}\quad t\in[0,T].

(6.3)

Proof.

Using (
1.5
), direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}|^{2}dydz-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}u_{0}\cdot\nabla c_{0}dydz

(6.4)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left[(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}+(n_{\neq}u_{\neq})_{0}\right]\cdot\left(\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right)dydz

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}|^{2}dydz+J_{1}+J_{2}.

For
j\in\{2,3\}
, by (
4.19
), we decompose
u_{j,0}(t,y,z)=\overline{u}_{j,0}(t)+\widetilde{u}_{j,0}(t,y,z)
, therefore

\displaystyle J_{1}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}u_{j,0}\partial_{j}c_{0}dydz

(6.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\overline{u}_{j,0}\partial_{j}c_{0}dydz-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\partial_{j}c_{0}dydz=:J_{11}+J_{12}.

For
J_{11},
using
(\ref{ini11})_{2}
and integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle J_{11}=\frac{1}{A}\overline{u}_{j,0}(t)\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left(\partial_{y}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2}\right)c_{0}\partial_{j}c_{0}dydz-\frac{1}{A}\overline{u}_{j,0}(t)\overline{n}(t)\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\partial_{j}c_{0}dydz=0.

For
J_{12},
due to
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\leq\|n_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{2}{3}}
, by Lemma
A.8
and H
\ddot{\rm o}
lder’s inequality, there holds

\displaystyle J_{12}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathbb{T}|}{A}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|n_{0}-\overline{n}\|_{L^{3}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A}m^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{5}{3}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining
J_{11}
and
J_{12}
via (
6.5
), we obtain that

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq\frac{C}{A}m^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{5}{3}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}.

For
J_{2}
, using Lemma
A.9
, (
6.1
), Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, one obtains

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\frac{|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}+(n_{\neq}u_{\neq})_{0}|^{2}}{n_{0}}dydz+\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\left|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right|^{2}dydz

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\left\|\frac{1}{n_{0}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\left|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right|^{2}dydz

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A\delta}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}t}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left(\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\left|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right|^{2}dydz.

Combining
J_{1}
and
J_{2},
we get by (
6.4
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\left|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right|^{2}dydz+\frac{C}{A}m^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{5}{3}}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2A\delta}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}t}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left(\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

which follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]-\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]

(6.6)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2A}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\left|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}\right|^{2}dydzds+\frac{C}{A}m^{\frac{1}{3}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2A\delta}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}s}\left(\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)ds.

Using Lemma
4.3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\|\widetilde{u}_{j,0}\|_{L^{2}}ds}{A}\leq\frac{C\epsilon\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{-\frac{s}{2A}}ds}{A}\leq C\epsilon.

(6.7)

Moreover, by Lemma
A.1
, Lemma
3.1
and assumptions (
2.5
), one deduces

{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\left(\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq CE_{2}^{2},

and
{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CE_{2}^{2}.
They imply that

\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{2}^{2}{\rm e}^{-2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t},\quad\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq CE_{2}^{2}{\rm e}^{-2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}.

Therefore, when
A
is sufficiently large satisfying
A\geq\left(\frac{\overline{n}}{a}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}s}\left(\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)ds\leq CE_{2}^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}{\rm e}^{\frac{\overline{n}}{A}s-2{a}{A^{-\frac{1}{3}}}s}ds\leq CE_{2}^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}{\rm e}^{-{a}{A^{-\frac{1}{3}}}s}ds=\frac{CE_{2}^{2}A^{\frac{1}{3}}}{a}.

Combining it with (
2.5
) and (
6.7
), (
6.6
) yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]-\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2A}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}|\nabla\log n_{0}-\nabla c_{0}|^{2}dydzds+C\epsilon m^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{\frac{5}{3}}+\frac{CE_{2}^{2}E_{3}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}a\delta}.

Hence, when

A\geq\max\left\{A_{2},A_{3},A_{4},\left(\frac{E_{2}^{2}E_{3}^{2}}{a\delta}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}},\left(\frac{\overline{n}}{a}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}},\left(\frac{1}{4a}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\right\}=:A_{5},

as long as
\epsilon
is enough small satisfying

\displaystyle\epsilon m^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{3}^{\frac{5}{3}}\leq C,

(6.8)

we obtain that

\mathcal{L}[n_{0}]-\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]\leq C.

∎

Next, as in
[
1
]
or
[
15
]
, we use (
6.3
) and (
A.6
) to get a bound on
\|n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}\|_{L^{1}}
.

Lemma 6.3
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
6.2
and

m=\|(n_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{1}}<8\pi,
there exists a constant
C_{L\log L}(n_{\rm in})
such that

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}dydz\leq C_{L\log L}(n_{\rm in}).

(6.9)

Proof.

Let
Y=(y,z)\in\mathbb{T}^{2}
be fixed. Define the cut-off function
\varphi(\tau)\in C^{\infty}
such that

\displaystyle{\rm supp}(\varphi)

\displaystyle=B(Y,1/4),

(6.10)

\displaystyle\varphi(\tau)

\displaystyle=1,\quad\forall\tau\in B(Y,1/8),

\displaystyle{\rm supp}\left(\nabla\varphi(\tau)\right)

\displaystyle\subset\overline{B}(Y,1/4)\backslash B(Y,1/8).

By periodically extending
n_{0}(\tau)
and
c_{0}(\tau)
to
\mathbb{R}^{2},
we can rewrite the equation
-\triangle c_{0}=n_{0}-\overline{n}
that holds on
\mathbb{T}^{2}
as the following equation that holds on
\mathbb{R}^{2}:

\displaystyle-\triangle_{\tau}\left(\varphi(\tau)c_{0}(\tau)\right)=

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\cdot\nabla_{\tau}c_{0}(\tau)-c_{0}(\tau)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)-\varphi(\tau)\triangle_{\tau}c_{0}(\tau)

(6.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\cdot\nabla_{\tau}c_{0}(\tau)-c_{0}(\tau)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)+\left(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n}\right)\varphi(\tau).

Since
\varphi(Y)=1
as
Y\in B(Y,1/8)
and
{\rm supp}(\varphi)=B(Y,1/4),
using (
6.11
) and the fundamental solution of the Laplacian on
\mathbb{R}^{2},
we get

\displaystyle c_{0}(Y)=c_{0}(Y)\varphi(Y)

(6.12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\log(|Y-\tau|)\left[(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n})\varphi(\tau)-2\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\cdot\nabla_{\tau}c_{0}(\tau)-c_{0}(\tau)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\right]d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{|Y-\tau|\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log(|Y-\tau|)(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n})\varphi(\tau)d\tau-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{|Y-\tau|\leq\frac{1}{4}}\nabla_{\tau}\cdot\left[\log(|Y-\tau|)\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\right]c_{0}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{|Y-\tau|\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log(|Y-\tau|)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)c_{0}(\tau)d\tau.

Due to the support of
\varphi,
we can identify the above with an analogous integral on
\mathbb{T}^{2}
with
|Y-\tau|
replaced by
d(Y,\tau).
Multiplying (
6.12
) by
-\frac{1}{2}(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n})
and integrating with respect to
Y
over
\mathbb{T}^{2},
we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)c_{0}(Y)dY

(6.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2},d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log d(Y,\tau)\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\left(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n}\right)\varphi(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2},\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\nabla_{\tau}\cdot\left(\log d(Y,\tau)\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\right)c_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2},\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\log d(Y,\tau)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)c_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY.

By using
(\ref{varphi})_{2},
one deduces that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log d(Y,\tau)\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\left(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n}\right)\varphi(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)>\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi}\overline{n}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)d\tau dY+\frac{1}{4\pi}(\overline{n})^{2}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log d(Y,\tau)\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\left(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n}\right)\varphi(\tau)d\tau dY.

Combining it with (
6.13
), we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)c_{0}(Y)dY

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)>\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi}\overline{n}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)d\tau dY+\frac{1}{4\pi}(\overline{n})^{2}\int\int_{d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{8}}\log d(Y,\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\log d(Y,\tau)\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\left(n_{0}(\tau)-\overline{n}\right)\varphi(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int\int_{\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\nabla_{\tau}\cdot\left(\log d(Y,\tau)\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\right)c_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\frac{1}{8}\leq d(Y,\tau)\leq\frac{1}{4}}\left(n_{0}(Y)-\overline{n}\right)\log d(Y,\tau)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)c_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY=:I_{1,1}+I_{1,2}+\cdots+I_{1,7}.

First of all, direct calculations show that

\displaystyle I_{1,2}+I_{1,3}+I_{1,4}+I_{1,5}\geq-Cm^{2}.

Moreover, in the region
\frac{1}{8}\leq|Y-\tau|\leq\frac{1}{4},
note that

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\tau}\cdot\left[\log(|Y-\tau|)\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)\right]\right|\leq 8|\nabla_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)|+\log 8|\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)|\leq C,

\displaystyle|\log(|Y-\tau|)\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)|\leq\log 8|\triangle_{\tau}\varphi(\tau)|\leq C,

we have

\displaystyle|I_{1,6}|+|I_{1,7}|\leq C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}(Y)dY\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}c_{0}(\tau)d\tau+C\overline{n}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}c_{0}(\tau)d\tau\leq Cm\|c_{0}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}.

Denoting
K(\tau)=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\log|\tau|
to be the fundamental solution of the Laplacian on
\mathbb{T}^{2},
as
-\triangle c_{0}=n_{0}-\overline{n},
by Young’s inequality, we obtain

\|c_{0}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}=\|K\ast(n_{0}-\overline{n})\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\leq\|K\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\|n_{0}-\overline{n}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\leq Cm.

This follows that

I_{1,6}+I_{1,7}\geq-Cm^{2}.

Combining the estimates of
I_{1,2}-I_{1,7},
we conclude that

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-\overline{n})c_{0}dY\geq\frac{1}{4\pi}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY-Cm^{2}.

From which along with (
6.3
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]\geq\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\frac{1}{2}(n_{0}-\overline{n})c_{0}dY-C

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY+\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY-Cm^{2}-C

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{m}{8\pi}\right)\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY

\displaystyle+\frac{m}{8\pi}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY+\frac{2}{m}\int\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{2}}\log d(Y,\tau)n_{0}(Y)n_{0}(\tau)d\tau dY\right)-Cm^{2}-C.

Applying (
A.6
) to it, one obtains

\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]\geq\left(1-\frac{m}{8\pi}\right)\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY-C(m)-Cm^{2},

which implies that

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY\leq\frac{\mathcal{L}[(n_{\rm in})_{0}]+C(m)+Cm^{2}}{1-\frac{m}{8\pi}}\leq C_{L\log L}(n_{\rm in})<\infty

under the condition of
m<8\pi.
Due to

\log^{-}n_{0}=-\min\{0,\log n_{0}\}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}-\log n_{0},&0<n_{0}<1,\\
0,&n_{0}\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

there holds

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}dY=

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}0,&0<n_{0}<1,\\
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log n_{0}dY,&n_{0}\geq 1.\end{array}\right.

This shows that

\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}dY\leq C_{L\log L}(n_{\rm in})<\infty.

The proof is complete.
∎

The following lemma gives a uniform in time
L^{2}
bound of
n_{0}.

Lemma 6.4
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
6.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|(n_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+m+1\right)=:H_{1}.

(6.14)

Proof.

Let
Q>\max\{1,\overline{n}\}
be a constant, to be chosen later. Noting that
(n_{0}-Q)_{+}=\max\{0,n_{0}-Q\},
and using (
6.9
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}dydz=\int_{n_{0}>Q}(n_{0}-Q)dydz\leq\int_{n_{0}>Q}n_{0}dydz

(6.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{n_{0}>Q}\frac{1}{\log^{+}n_{0}}n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}dydz\leq\frac{1}{\log Q}\int_{n_{0}>Q}n_{0}\log^{+}n_{0}dydz\leq\frac{C_{L\log L}}{\log Q}.

Recall that
n_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\partial_{t}n_{0}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\triangle n_{0}-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(n_{0}\nabla c_{0})-\frac{1}{A}\nabla\cdot(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}-\frac{1}{A}(u_{0}\cdot\nabla n_{0})-\frac{1}{A}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla n_{\neq})_{0}.

(6.16)

As
(n_{0}-Q)_{+}(n_{0}-Q)_{-}=0,
multiplying (
6.16
) by
(n_{0}-Q)_{+}
and integrating with respect to
(y,z)
over
\mathbb{T}^{2},
we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{A}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\triangle n_{0}dydz-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\nabla\cdot(n_{0}\nabla c_{0})dydz

(6.17)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\nabla\cdot(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}dydz-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}(u_{0}\cdot\nabla n_{0})dydz

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla n_{\neq})_{0}dydz\Big)=:J_{1}+\cdots+J_{5}.

For
J_{1},
integration by parts shows that

J_{1}=-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|\nabla((n_{0}-Q)_{+})|^{2}dydz.

For
J_{2},
using
(\ref{ini11})
and integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle J_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)^{2}(n_{0}-\overline{n})dydz+\frac{Q}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}(n_{0}-\overline{n})dydz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)^{3}dydz+\frac{3Q-\overline{n}}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)^{2}dydz+\frac{Q^{2}-Q\overline{n}}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}dydz

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)^{3}dydz+\frac{3Q}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)^{2}dydz+\frac{Q^{2}m}{A}.

For
J_{3}
and
J_{5},
by integration by parts, one deduces

J_{3}+J_{5}\leq\frac{1}{16A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)|^{2}dydz+\frac{C(\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(n_{\neq}u_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})}{A}.

For
J_{4},
using
\nabla\cdot u_{0}=0
, for
j\in\{2,3\},
we have

\displaystyle J_{4}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}u_{j,0}\partial_{j}n_{0}dydz=-\frac{1}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}u_{j,0}\partial_{j}(n_{0}-Q)_{+}dydz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}u_{j,0}\partial_{j}((n_{0}-Q)_{+})^{2}dydz=0.

Collecting
J_{1}-J_{5},
we get by (
6.17
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{7\|\nabla(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{8A}+\frac{\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{3}}^{3}}{2A}+\frac{3Q\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}}{2A}

(6.18)

\displaystyle+\frac{Q^{2}m}{A}+\frac{C}{A}\left(\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Using Lemma
A.7
and (
6.15
), one obtains

\displaystyle\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{3}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{L\log L}}{\log Q}\|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}.

Thus, we choose
Q
depending only on
C_{L\log L}
such that

\displaystyle-\frac{7}{8A}\|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}+\frac{1}{2A}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{3}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{3}\leq-\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}.

(6.19)

Similarly, it follows from Lemma
A.7
and (
6.15
) that

\displaystyle-\|\nabla\left((n_{0}-Q)_{+}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}\leq-\frac{\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{4}}{C\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}}\leq-\frac{1}{Cm^{2}}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{4}.

(6.20)

Substituting (
6.19
) and (
6.20
) into (
6.18
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{ACm^{2}}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{4}+\frac{3Q}{A}\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}

(6.21)

\displaystyle+\frac{2Q^{2}m}{A}+\frac{C}{A}\left(\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}+\|(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}\right).

We denote
G(t)
by

G(t):=\frac{C}{A}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|(n_{\neq}\nabla c_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}+\|(u_{\neq}n_{\neq})_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}^{2}\right)ds,\quad{\rm for}\quad t\geq 0.

Then using Lemma
A.1
, Lemma
A.9
, Lemma
3.1
and assumption (
2.5
), direct calculations indicate that

\displaystyle G(t)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A}\left(\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|u_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(6.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\|n\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}n_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\triangle u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\right)\leq\frac{CE_{2}^{2}E_{3}^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\leq C

provided with
A\geq A_{5}.
Moreover, using Young’s inequality, we rewrite
(\ref{n0-Q 2})
as

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-G(t)\right)\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\left[\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-G(t)-\left(9C^{2}m^{4}Q^{2}+4Cm^{3}Q^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]}{2ACm^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left[\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\left(9C^{2}m^{4}Q^{2}+4Cm^{3}Q^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right],

which implies that

\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-G(t)\leq\|(n_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\left(9C^{2}m^{4}Q^{2}+4Cm^{3}Q^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining it with (
6.22
), one deduces

\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|(n_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+m+1\right).

(6.23)

By decomposing
n_{0}=(n_{0}-Q)_{+}+\min\{n_{0},Q\}
and using (
6.23
), we get

\displaystyle\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\min\{n_{0},Q\}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(n_{0}-Q)_{+}\|_{L^{2}}+Q^{\frac{1}{2}}m^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\left(\|(n_{\rm in})_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+m+1\right),

which gives the result.

The proof is complete.
∎

7.
Estimates for
L^{\infty}
-norm of the density
E_{3}(t)
: Proof of Proposition
2.2

Proof of Proposition
2.2
.

For
p=2^{j}
with
j\geq 1,
multiplying
(\ref{ini11})_{1}
by
2pn^{2p-1}
, and integrating by parts the resulting equation over
\mathbb{T}^{3}
, one deduces

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{2(2p-1)}{Ap}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=\frac{2(2p-1)}{A}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}n^{p}\nabla c\cdot\nabla n^{p}dxdydz

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2(2p-1)}{A}\|n^{p}\nabla c\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{2p-1}{Ap}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{(2p-1)p}{A}\|n^{p}\nabla c\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Hölder’s inequality and Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|n^{p}\nabla c\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|n^{p}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\leq C\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2},

which follows that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{2(2p-1)}{Ap}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2p-1}{Ap}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C(2p-1)p}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\frac{C(2p-1)p}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{5(2p-1)}{4Ap}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C(2p-1)p^{7}}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{8}+\frac{C(2p-1)p}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{2}.

Consequently, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2A}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{Cp^{8}}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(1+\|\nabla c\|_{L^{4}}^{8}\right).

(7.1)

Using Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality

\displaystyle\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|n^{p}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{2}{5}}\|\nabla n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{5}}+\|n^{p}\|_{L^{1}}\right),

we infer from (
7.1
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq-\frac{\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{10}{3}}}{2AC\|n^{p}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{4}{3}}}+\frac{Cp^{8}}{A}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(1+\|\nabla c\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}^{8}\right).

Applying Lemma
A.8
, Lemma
A.9
, Lemma
6.4
and the assumption (
1.6
), there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla c\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}+\|\nabla c_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\left(E_{2}+H_{1}\right).

Therefore

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq-\frac{\|n^{p}\|^{\frac{10}{3}}_{L^{2}}}{2CA\|n^{p}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{4}{3}}}+\frac{Cp^{8}}{A}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}(1+E_{2}^{8}+H_{1}^{8}),

which indicates that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\|n^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\max\left\{8C^{3}(1+E_{2}^{8}+H_{1}^{8})^{\frac{3}{2}}p^{12}\sup_{t\geq 0}\|n^{p}\|^{2}_{L^{1}},2\|n_{\rm in}^{p}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right\}.

(7.2)

Next, the Moser-Alikakos iteration is used to determine
E_{3}
. Recall
p=2^{j}
with
j\geq 1,

and we rewrite (
7.2
) into

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{j+1}}dxdydz\leq\max\Big\{C_{1}p^{12}\Big(\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{j}}dxdydz\Big)^{2},2\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n_{\rm in}|^{2^{j+1}}dxdydz\Big\},

(7.3)

where
C_{1}=8C^{3}(1+E_{2}^{8}+H_{1}^{8})^{\frac{3}{2}}.

From Lemma
6.4
, we note that

\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq H_{1}.

Hence

\sup_{t\geq 0}\|n(t)\|_{L^{2}}\leq|\mathbb{T}|\|n_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|n_{\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq|\mathbb{T}|H_{1}+E_{2}.

By interpolation inequality, for
0<\theta<1
, we have

\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{2^{j}}}\leq\|n_{\rm in}\|^{\theta}_{L^{2}}\|n_{\rm in}\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{2}}+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq|\mathbb{T}|H_{1}+E_{2}+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}}

for
j\geq 1.
This yields that

2\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n_{\rm in}|^{2^{j+1}}dxdydz\leq 2\left(|\mathbb{T}|H_{1}+E_{2}+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)^{2^{j+1}}\leq K^{2^{j+1}},

where
K=2(|\mathbb{T}|H_{1}+E_{2}+\|n_{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Now, we rewrite (
7.3
) as

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{j+1}}dxdydz\leq\max\left\{C_{1}4096^{j}\left(\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{j}}dxdydz\right)^{2},K^{2^{j+1}}\right\}.

For
j=k
, we get

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{k+1}}dxdydz\leq C_{1}^{a_{k}}4096^{b_{k}}K^{2^{k+1}},

where
a_{k}=1+2a_{k-1}
and
b_{k}=k+2b_{k-1}
.

Generally, one can obtain the following formulas

a_{k}=2^{k}-1,\ {\rm and}\ \ b_{k}=2^{k+1}-k-2.

Therefore, one deduces

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\left(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|n(t)|^{2^{k+1}}dxdydz\right)^{\frac{1}{2^{k+1}}}\leq C_{1}^{\frac{2^{k}-1}{2^{k+1}}}4096^{\frac{2^{k+1}-k-2}{2^{k+1}}}K.

Letting
k\rightarrow\infty
, there holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\|n(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(1+E_{2}^{8}+H_{1}^{8})^{\frac{3}{4}}(|\mathbb{T}|H_{1}+E_{2}+||n_{\rm in}||_{L^{\infty}})=:E_{3}.

(7.4)

∎

8.
Energy estimates for
E_{4}(t)
: Proof of Proposition
2.3

To estimate
\|\partial^{2}_{x}u_{2,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}
and

\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}},
it is important to
introduce the new quantity
W
defined by

W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq},

where

V=y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A},\quad{\rm and}~~\kappa=\frac{\partial_{z}V}{\partial_{y}V}.

(8.1)

The similar quality was first proposed by Wei-Zhang in
[
34
]
and further applied in
[
5
]
and
[
8
]
.

For
j\in\{2,3\},
there holds

(u\cdot\nabla u_{j})_{\neq}=u_{0}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq}+u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0}+(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})_{\neq}.

Then we infer from (
1.3
) that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+{G_{2,3}}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}(P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})}{A}=0,\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{3,\neq}+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+{G_{3,3}}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{z}(P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{2}}_{\neq})}{A}=0,\end{array}\right.

(8.2)

where
\mathcal{L}_{V}
can be found in (
5.10
) and

\displaystyle g_{j,1}={u_{2,0}\partial_{y}u_{j,\neq}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}u_{j,\neq}},\quad g_{j,2}={u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,0}},\quad G_{j,3}={(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{j,\neq})_{\neq}}.

(8.3)

Due to
{\rm div}~u=0,
we have

\displaystyle{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{\neq}

\displaystyle=\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla u_{1})_{\neq}+\partial_{y}(u\cdot\nabla u_{2})_{\neq}+\partial_{z}(u\cdot\nabla u_{3})_{\neq}

\displaystyle={\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}+2(\partial_{y}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}u_{1,0}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})+2\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+2\partial_{z}g_{3,2},

which along with
\partial_{y}V=1+\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}
implies that

\displaystyle\frac{P^{N_{1}}_{\neq}+P^{N_{2}}_{\neq}}{A}=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\left(\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{{\rm div}~(u\cdot\nabla u)_{\neq}}{2A}-\frac{\partial_{x}n_{\neq}}{2A}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\Bigg(\left(1+\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}+\frac{{\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}}{2A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}}{A}-\frac{\partial_{x}n_{\neq}}{2A}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\triangle^{-1}\left(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W+\frac{\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}}{A}+\frac{P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3}}{A}\right),

where

P_{1,1}=\frac{{\rm div}~(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})_{\neq}}{2},\quad P_{1,2}=\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\quad P_{1,3}=-\frac{\partial_{x}n_{\neq}}{2}.

(8.4)

Using the above decomposition, we rewrite (
8.2
) into

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}&\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{2,\neq}-2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+{G_{2,3}}}{A}=\frac{2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2})}{A}\\
&\qquad+\frac{2\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})}{A},\\
&\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}}{A}+\frac{g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+{G_{3,3}}}{A}=\frac{2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2})}{A}\\
&\qquad+\frac{2\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})}{A}.\end{array}\right.

(8.5)

Therefore
W=u_{2,\neq}+\kappa u_{3,\neq}
satisfies

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W}{A}+\frac{G^{(1)}+G^{(2)}}{A}=\left(\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A}\right)u_{3,\neq}-\frac{2\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}}{A},

(8.6)

where

\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W=\mathcal{L}_{V}W-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)

and

\displaystyle G^{(1)}=G_{2,3}+\kappa G_{3,3}-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3}),

\displaystyle G^{(2)}=g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+\kappa(g_{3,1}+g_{3,2})-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}).

In addition,
\triangle W
satisfies

\mathcal{L}_{V}\triangle W=\triangle\left(-\frac{2\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}}{A}\right)+{\rm good}~{\rm terms}.

To remove the singular term
\triangle\left(-\frac{2\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}}{A}\right)
,
motivated by the quasi-linear method in
[
34
]
, we introduce the following decomposition

\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}=\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq},

(8.7)

where

\rho_{1}=\frac{\partial_{y}\kappa+\kappa\partial_{z}\kappa}{\partial_{y}V(1+\kappa^{2})},\quad\rho_{2}=\frac{\partial_{z}\kappa-\kappa\partial_{y}\kappa}{1+\kappa^{2}}.

(8.8)

As
(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})
has a good commutative relation with
\mathcal{L}_{V},
it is a good derivative. Thus the second term in (
8.7
) is good. To handle the first term in (
8.7
) and obtain a sharp threshold of velocity, we need to make a further decomposition for
W
as follows

W=W^{(1)}+\frac{1}{A}W^{(2)},

where
W^{(1)}
and
W^{(2)}
solve

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}\triangle W^{(1)}={\rm good}~{\rm terms},\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}=-\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq},\\
W^{(1)}(0)=W(0),~W^{(2)}(0)=0.\end{array}\right.

Furthermore, an additional quantity
W^{(3)}
is also needed satisfying

\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(3)}=-\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq},\quad W^{(3)}(0)=0.

We denote

\triangle u_{3,\neq}=\left(\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\right)+2\partial_{x}W^{(3)},

which satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\left(\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\right)=

\displaystyle 2\partial_{z}\left(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W\right)-\frac{\triangle(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})}{A}-\frac{\triangle(g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+G_{3,3})}{A}

(8.9)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\partial_{z}\left(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}\right)}{A}+\frac{2\partial_{z}\left(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3}\right)}{A}-\triangle V\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}

and

\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(3)}=-\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}.

(8.10)

In this way, by space-time estimates, we will prove that

E_{4}^{2}(t)\leq E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|u_{\rm in}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\right),

where
E_{5,1}^{2}(t)
and
E_{5,2}^{2}(t)
are the auxiliary norms defined by

\displaystyle E_{5,1}(t)=A^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}},

\displaystyle E_{5,2}(t)=\sum_{j=2}^{3}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{X_{b}}.

Lemma 8.1
.

Under the result of Lemma
4.5
, it holds that

\|\kappa\|_{H^{1}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon,\quad\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon,

\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|_{H^{1}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\epsilon,\quad\|\partial_{t}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\epsilon,

\|\rho_{1}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\rho_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon,

where the definitions of
\kappa,\rho_{1}
and
\rho_{2}
are given by (
8.1
) and (
8.8
).

The following lemma gives the estimates of
W^{(2)}
and
W^{(3)}
associated with good derivatives.

Lemma 8.2
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
1.1
and the assumptions (
2.5
), it holds that

(i)

\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t),

(ii)
