\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t),\quad\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t),

(iii)

\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Proof.

Estimate (i).

Applying Proposition
A.3
to (
8.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}(\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.11)

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})(\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big)=:CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(I_{1}+I_{2}\right).

Recalling that
V=y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A},
by Lemma
4.5
and Lemma
8.1
,
we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1+A^{-1}\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left(1+A^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\right)\leq C,

(8.12)

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq CA^{-1}\left(1+\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}\right)\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C.

(8.13)

Then for
I_{1},
using (
8.12
), we have

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CAE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

For
I_{2},
using (
8.12
) and (
8.13
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\left(\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

which implies

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle CA\left(\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)\leq CAE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Combining the estimates of
I_{1}
and
I_{2},
(
8.11
) gives the result of (i).

Estimate (ii).
Notice that

\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}=\partial_{x}^{2}\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}=-\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}

and
\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}(0)=0.
Then
applying Proposition
A.1
, there holds

\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

Using Lemma
8.1
and (
8.12
), we obtain

\|\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\rho_{1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

This indicates that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

(8.14)

For
j\in\{1,2,3\},
we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{x}\partial_{j}W^{(2)}=

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\partial_{j}\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}-\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}=-\partial_{j}\left(\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\right)-\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}

satisfying
\partial_{x}\partial_{j}W^{(2)}(0)=0.
Applying Proposition
A.1
to it, one deduces

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\partial_{j}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+CA\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

(8.15)

Due to (
8.12
) and Lemma
8.1
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{j}V\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\rho_{1}\|_{H^{2}}\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}.

Substituting the above estimations into (
8.15
) and using (
8.14
), we arrive at

\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+C\epsilon^{2}A^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}A^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Estimate (iii).

Due to

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}(\rho_{1}f)-\rho_{1}\mathcal{L}_{V}f=

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\rho_{1}-A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1})f-2A^{-1}\nabla\rho_{1}\cdot\nabla f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1})f-2A^{-1}\nabla\cdot(f\nabla\rho_{1})

and
\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}=\rho_{1}\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(3)},
there holds

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}\partial_{x}\left(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)}\right)=

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\mathcal{L}_{V}\left(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)}\right)=\partial_{x}\left(\rho_{1}\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(3)}-\mathcal{L}_{V}(\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}\left[(\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1})W^{(3)}-2A^{-1}\nabla\cdot(W^{(3)}\nabla\rho_{1})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\triangle Q+2A^{-1}\nabla\cdot\left(\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\nabla\rho_{1}\right),

where
\triangle Q=\left(\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1}\right)\partial_{x}W^{(3)}.

Applying Proposition
A.1
, we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\left(A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla Q\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\nabla\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(8.16)

It follows from Lemma
8.1
that
\|\nabla\rho_{1}\|_{H^{1}}\leq\|\rho_{1}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon
and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{t}\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+A^{-1}\|\rho_{1}\|_{H^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\epsilon.

Combining them with Lemma
A.5
, one obtains

\displaystyle\|\nabla Q\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}\triangle Q\|_{L^{2}}=\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}[\left(\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1}\right)\partial_{x}W^{(3)}]\|_{L^{2}}

(8.17)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{t}\rho_{1}+A^{-1}\triangle\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\left(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y}\right)W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-1}\epsilon\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\nabla\rho_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\rho_{1}\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

(8.18)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Substituting (
8.17
) and (
8.18
) into (
8.16
), and using the result of (i), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-1}\epsilon^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-1}\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

∎

Lemma 8.3
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and (
2.5
), it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)\left(A^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\right),

(8.19)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)AE_{5,2}^{2}(t),

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}^{2}(u_{2,\neq},u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{1}{3}(2\alpha+1)}E_{5,2}^{2}(t),

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t),

where
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4})
and the definition of
H_{1}
is the same as in Lemma
6.4
.

Proof.

Estimate
(\ref{ineq:U1})_{1}.
Recalling that
W=W^{(1)}+\frac{1}{A}W^{(2)},
we have

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W^{(1)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W^{(2)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

Using Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
, Lemma
A.2
, Lemma
A.3

and

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla f\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}=\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}\partial_{x}(\triangle f)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq\|\triangle f\|_{X_{b}},

one obtains

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W^{(1)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.20)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,z}L^{2}_{y}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,z}L^{\infty}_{y}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}L^{\infty}_{y,z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2},

where
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4}).

Similarly, by Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
, Lemma
A.2
, Lemma
A.3
and (ii) of Lemma
8.2
, there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W^{(2)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.21)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\triangle W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{3-2\alpha}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\triangle W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2\alpha-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2})A^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2})\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{8}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Collecting (
8.20
) and (
8.21
), we get the first result.

Estimate
(\ref{ineq:U1})_{2}
.

By Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
, Lemma
A.2
and Lemma
A.4
, we arrive

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}L^{\infty}_{y,z}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,y}L^{2}_{z}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,y}L^{\infty}_{z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{2}}t}\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)AE_{5,2}^{2}(t),

where we use Lemma
8.1
and
(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}=\nabla(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}+\nabla\kappa\partial_{y}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}.

Estimate
(\ref{ineq:U1})_{3}.
Due to Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
, Lemma
A.2
and Lemma
A.3
, for
j\in\{2,3\}
and
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4}),
there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{x,y}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2-2\alpha}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{1}{3}(2\alpha+1)}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Estimate
(\ref{ineq:U1})_{4}.
According to
P_{1,2}=\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{2,\neq}+\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}
in (
8.4
), using Lemma
4.4
, Lemma
6.4
and Lemma
A.4
, for
j\in\{2,3\},
we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{j}\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{y,z}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

∎

Lemma 8.4
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and (
2.5
), it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}}\epsilon^{2}\left(E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\right),

(8.22)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}g_{3,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{2,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Proof.

Estimate
(\ref{estimate:g})_{1}.

By Lemma
4.1
, Lemma
A.2
and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0},
we get

\|\nabla(u_{2,0},u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq CA\epsilon^{2},

(8.23)

which along with (
3.19
) and

\|\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq A^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

shows that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}=\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}u_{3,\neq}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u_{2,0},u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|(u_{2,0},u_{3,0})\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA\epsilon^{2}\|\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}}\epsilon^{2}\big(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+A^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\big)\leq CA^{\frac{5}{3}}\epsilon^{2}\left(E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+E_{5,1}^{2}(t)\right).

Estimate
(\ref{estimate:g})_{2}.

As

g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1}=\left(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z}\right)W-u_{3,\neq}(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z})\kappa,

by (
3.19
) and (
8.23
), we get

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA\epsilon^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla W\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{3,\neq}\nabla\kappa\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\right).

Direct calculations show that

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla W\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

On the other hand, using Lemma
8.1
, one obtains

\displaystyle\|u_{3,\neq}\nabla\kappa\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u_{3,\neq}\nabla\kappa)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|u_{3,\neq}\nabla\kappa\|_{H^{1}}\leq C\|u_{3,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla\kappa\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}},

which indicates that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{3,\neq}\nabla\kappa\|_{Y_{0}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

This gives

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

(8.24)

Recall that
\partial_{x}g_{3,1}=(u_{2,0}\partial_{y}+u_{3,0}\partial_{z})\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}.
By (
3.19
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}g_{3,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|(u_{2,0},u_{3,0})\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

(8.25)

For
g_{2,2}=u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,0}
and
j\in\{2,3\},
it follows from Lemma
A.5
and Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla g_{2,2}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla\left(u_{j,\neq}\partial_{j}u_{2,0}\right)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{j}u_{2,0}\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|u_{j,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}+\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u_{2,0}\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{2,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(8.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}A\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

For
g_{3,2}=u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,0},
we rewrite it into

g_{3,2}=\left(u_{2,\neq}\partial_{y}+u_{3,\neq}\partial_{z}\right)u_{3,0}=W\partial_{y}u_{3,0}+u_{3,\neq}\left(\partial_{z}u_{3,0}-\kappa\partial_{y}u_{3,0}\right),

which implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(W\partial_{y}u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left(u_{3,\neq}(\partial_{z}u_{3,0}-\kappa\partial_{y}u_{3,0})\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

(8.27)

By Lemma
A.2
, Lemma
A.3
, Lemma
4.1
and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0}
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(W\partial_{y}u_{3,0})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla W\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{y}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,y}L^{2}_{x,z}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{y}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t,y}L^{\infty}_{z}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}^{2}+\|u_{2,0}\|_{L^{2}H^{3}}^{2}\right)\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}W\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

As
\mathbf{U}_{2}(0)=0,
using Lemma
4.5
, there holds

\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\mathbf{U}_{2}(t)\|_{H^{2}}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}(s)\|_{H^{2}}ds\leq C\epsilon t.

On the other hand,
\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}(t)\|_{H^{2}}\leq CA\epsilon.
Therefore

\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon A\min\{A^{-1}t,1\},

which implies that

\|\kappa\|_{H^{1}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon\min\{A^{-1}t,1\}.

(8.29)

From this, along with Lemma
4.1
and Lemma
8.1
, one obtains

\displaystyle\|\kappa\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{H^{1}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\kappa\|_{H^{2}}\left(\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

(8.30)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\kappa\|_{H^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\min\{A^{-1}t,1\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\left(\|\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\min\left(A^{-\frac{2}{3}}+A^{-1}t,1\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\triangle u_{3,0}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\leq C\epsilon^{2}.

Due to Lemma
4.1
, Lemma
A.5
, (
8.30
) and
\partial_{z}u_{3,0}=-\partial_{y}u_{2,0}
, we arrive

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left(u_{3,\neq}(\partial_{z}u_{3,0}-\kappa\partial_{y}u_{3,0})\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.31)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\partial_{z}u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\kappa\nabla u_{3,0}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\right)\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Combining (
8.28
) with (
8.31
), we get from (
8.27
) that

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

(8.32)

Then
(\ref{estimate:g})_{2}
follows from (
8.24
), (
8.25
), (
8.26
) and (
8.32
).

∎

Lemma 8.5
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and (
2.5
), it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{6}+\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t),

where
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4}).

Proof.

Due to (
8.3
) and Lemma
3.2
, there holds

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G_{2,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha}E_{2}^{4}.

For
G_{3,3}=(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}
in (
8.3
), using Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
8.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\kappa G_{3,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

(8.33)

On the other hand, by Lemma
8.1
and (
8.29
), one deduces

\displaystyle\|\kappa\nabla G_{3,3}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\kappa\|_{H^{2}}\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\kappa\|_{H^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(A^{-1}t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}},

which along with Lemma
3.2
and
(A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C{\rm e}^{(2a-b)A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}
implies that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\kappa\nabla G_{3,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(A^{-\frac{1}{3}}t)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.34)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}+CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}.

It follows from (
8.33
) and (
8.34
) that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\kappa G_{3,3})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}.

Using Lemma
3.2
, we note that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{1,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{2,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big)\leq CA^{\frac{1}{6}+\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha}E_{2}^{4}.

From this, along with (
8.4
),
(\ref{ineq:U1})_{4}
and Lemma
8.1
, one obtains

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left((\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.35)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}P_{1,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{6}+\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+C(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2})A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{2}.

Based on the above estimates, we conclude that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle CA^{\frac{1}{6}+\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{2}{3}\alpha}E_{2}^{4}+CA^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{2}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}

(8.36)

\displaystyle+C\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{2}.

For
G^{(2)}=g_{2,1}+g_{2,2}+\kappa(g_{3,1}+g_{3,2})-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2}),
by Lemma
8.1
and Lemma
8.4
, there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{2,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.37)

\displaystyle+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla g_{3,2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big)\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Combining (
8.36
) with (
8.37
), we finish the proof.

∎

Lemma 8.6
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and (
2.5
), there exists a positive constant
A_{6}
independent of
t
and
A,
such that if
A\geq A_{6},
then there holds

\displaystyle E_{4}(t)+E_{5,1}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}+1\right)=:E_{4}+E_{5}.

Proof.

Step I. Estimate
\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}.

Applying Proposition
A.1
to (
8.9
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}

(8.38)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|(\triangle u_{3,{\rm in}})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle V\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,2}+g_{3,1}+g_{3,2}+G_{3,3})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{A}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big),

where we use
\partial_{x}W^{(3)}(0)=0.
Due to
\|\triangle V\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq CA^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C,
there holds

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle V\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\triangle V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.39)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Moreover, for
\|\partial_{y}V\|_{H^{3}}\leq C\left(1+A^{-1}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\right)\leq C,
one deduces

\displaystyle\|\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{y}V\|_{H^{3}}\|\partial_{x}W\|_{H^{1}}\leq C\|\nabla\partial_{x}W\|_{L^{2}},

(8.40)

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{z}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}W\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Recall that
G_{3,3}=(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq})_{\neq}
in (
8.3
), by Lemma
3.2
, and we get

\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla G_{3,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(A^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{7}{6}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}+A^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{2}^{4}\right),

(8.41)

where
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4}).

Collecting (
8.39
)-(
8.41
), and using (
8.35
), Lemma
8.3
and Lemma
8.4
, we get from (
8.38
) that

\displaystyle\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+A^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{6}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{4}+\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+1\right)

(8.42)

provided with
A\geq\max\{A_{5},E_{2}^{\frac{24}{5-6\alpha}},E_{2}^{\frac{24}{3-4\alpha}},(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}\}=:A_{6,1}.

Step II. Estimate
\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}.

By (
8.6
), there holds

\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W}{A}+\frac{G^{(1)}+G^{(2)}}{A}=\left(\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A}\right)u_{3,\neq}-\frac{2\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}}{A}.

Using the following decomposition

\nabla\kappa\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}=\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}

and
W=W^{(1)}+\frac{1}{A}W^{(2)},
we get

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W^{(1)}+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W}{A}+\frac{G^{(1)}+G^{(2)}}{A}-\left(\partial_{t}\kappa-\frac{\triangle\kappa}{A}\right)u_{3,\neq}

(8.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{A}\left(\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}+\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\right)-\frac{1}{A}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W^{(2)}=-\frac{2}{A}\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}-\frac{1}{A}J_{11},

where

J_{11}=\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}W^{(2)}+2\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}.

(8.44)

Applying Proposition
A.4
to (
8.43
), one deduces

\displaystyle\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\triangle W^{(1)}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left(\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(8.45)

\displaystyle+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla J_{11}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla((\partial_{t}\kappa-A^{-1}\triangle\kappa)u_{3,\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}W)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(G^{(1)}+G^{(2)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big).

It follows from Lemma
8.1
that
\|\rho_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C\epsilon
and

\|\partial_{t}\kappa-A^{-1}\triangle\kappa\|_{H^{1}}\leq\|\partial_{t}\kappa\|_{H^{1}}+CA^{-1}\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}\leq CA^{-1}\epsilon.

(8.46)

Hence we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\left(\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\rho_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\|\nabla(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon\|\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}.

(8.47)

Besides, using (
8.46
) and Lemma
A.5
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla\left((\partial_{t}\kappa-A^{-1}\triangle\kappa)u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}}\leq CA^{-1}\epsilon\left(\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

(8.48)

For
J_{11}
in (
8.44
), we rewrite it as follows:

\displaystyle J_{11}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}W^{(2)}-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})+2\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}

(8.49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})+\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}VJ_{12})-(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\triangle u_{3,\neq})+\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq},

where
J_{12}=2\partial_{x}W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}\triangle u_{3,\neq}=2\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})-\rho_{1}\left(\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\right).

Using Lemma
8.1
and (
8.12
), we arrive

\displaystyle J_{12}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{L^{2}}+\epsilon\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}VJ_{12})\|_{H^{1}}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}VJ_{12})\|_{H^{1}}+\|\kappa\|_{H^{3}}\|\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}VJ_{12})\|_{H^{1}}\right)

(8.50)

\displaystyle\leq C\|J_{12}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{L^{2}}+\epsilon\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Thanks to
\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}=\partial_{y}V(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})u_{3,\neq},
there holds

\displaystyle-(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\triangle u_{3,\neq})+\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left[(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1},\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\right]\triangle u_{3,\neq}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left[\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1},\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\right]\triangle u_{3,\neq}-\kappa\left[\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1},\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\right]\triangle u_{3,\neq}.

From this, along with (
8.12
), Lemma
8.1
and Lemma
A.5
, we get

\displaystyle\|-(\partial_{y}+\kappa\partial_{z})\triangle^{-1}\left(\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\triangle u_{3,\neq}\right)+\rho_{1}\nabla V\cdot\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}

(8.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\left[\partial_{y}\triangle^{-1},\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\right]\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\left[\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1},\partial_{y}V\rho_{1}\right]\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\left(\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\nabla u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Then we conclude from (
8.49
), (
8.50
) and (
8.51
) that

\displaystyle\|\nabla J_{11}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\partial_{x}(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)})\|_{L^{2}}+\epsilon\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

(8.52)

\displaystyle+C\epsilon\left(\|\nabla\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Collecting (
8.42
), (
8.47
), (
8.48
), (
8.52
) and (iii) of Lemma
8.2
, when
A\geq A_{6,1},
one obtains

\displaystyle A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla J_{11}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left(\rho_{2}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\left((\partial_{t}\kappa-\nu\triangle\kappa)u_{3,\neq}\right)\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CA^{-\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|\partial_{x}\left(W^{(2)}-\rho_{1}W^{(3)}\right)\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+1\right),

Combining above with Lemma
8.3
and Lemma
8.5
, when

A\geq C\max\{\epsilon^{-6}(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2})^{3},(\epsilon^{-1}E_{2})^{\frac{12}{3-2\alpha}},(E_{2}E_{5})^{\frac{12}{3-2\alpha}},A_{6,1}\}=:A_{6,2},

we get from (
8.45
) that

\displaystyle\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+1\right)+CA^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}.

This implies that

\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+1\right).

(8.53)

Step III. Estimate
\|\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}.

As
W=W^{(1)}+\frac{1}{A}W^{(2)},
for
j\in\{2,3\},
we rewrite (
8.5
) into

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}u_{j,\neq}=

\displaystyle 2\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(1)})+\frac{2}{A}\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(2)})-\frac{\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}}{A}-\frac{g_{j,1}+g_{j,2}+G_{j,3}}{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}(\partial_{y}g_{2,2}+\partial_{z}g_{3,2})}{A}+\frac{2\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})}{A}.

Applying Proposition
A.3
to it, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}

(8.54)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(1)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\mathbf{U}_{1}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{j,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(g_{j,1}+G_{j,3})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,2}+g_{3,2})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{-1}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}(P_{1,1}+P_{1,2}+P_{1,3})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big),

where we use

\|\partial_{x}^{2}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(1+\|\kappa\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}\nabla f\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}\nabla f\|_{L^{2}}.

By Lemma
4.5
, we arrive

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{j}(\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}W^{(1)})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\partial_{x}W^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(1+\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}}{A}\right)\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq C\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}

and

A^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq A^{-\frac{5}{3}}\|\partial_{y}V\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}.

Due to Lemma
8.1
and Lemma
8.4
, one obtains

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}g_{j,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla(g_{2,1}+\kappa g_{3,1})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}g_{3,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\epsilon^{2}AE_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Moreover, it follows from Lemma
3.2
that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{bA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}G_{j,3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{2aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(u_{\neq}\cdot\nabla u_{\neq})\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq CA^{\frac{1}{6}+\alpha}E_{2}^{4}.

By combining the above estimates and using Lemma
8.3
, Lemma
8.4
and (
8.35
),
when

A\geq C\max\{\epsilon^{\frac{-3}{1-\alpha}}\left(\|(u_{1,\rm in})_{0}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+H_{1}^{2}\right)^{\frac{3}{2(1-\alpha)}},A_{6,2}\}=:A_{6}.

we get from (
8.54
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})u_{j,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}

(8.55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+A^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+1\right).

Step IV. Estimate
E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)
and
E_{4}^{2}(t).

For
W=W^{(1)}+\frac{1}{A}W^{(2)},
there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}\nabla W\|_{X_{b}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}+\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}\leq\frac{1}{A}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}+\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}},

which along with (
8.55
) gives

\displaystyle E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|\triangle W^{(1)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+A^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+A^{-2}\|\partial_{x}\nabla W^{(2)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+1\big).

By (
8.53
) and (ii) of Lemma
8.2
, the above inequality indicates that

E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+1\right)+C\epsilon^{2}E_{5,2}^{2}(t).

Taking
\epsilon
small enough satisfying
C\epsilon^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2},
we have

\displaystyle E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+1\right).

(8.56)

Using (
8.42
) and (i) of Lemma
8.2
, one deduces

\displaystyle\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}-2\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}W^{(3)}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}\right)

(8.57)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{4}{3}}E_{5,2}^{2}(t)+A^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{1}{6}}E_{2}^{2}E_{5}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}E_{2}^{4}+\epsilon^{2}A^{\frac{2}{3}}E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+1\right).

Combining (
8.56
) and (
8.57
), when
A\geq A_{6},
there holds

E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)=A^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\triangle u_{3,\neq}\|_{X_{b}}^{2}+E_{5,2}^{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\right)+C\epsilon^{2}E_{5,1}^{2}(t).

Choosing
\epsilon
small enough satisfying
C\epsilon^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we conclude that

E_{4}^{2}(t)\leq C(E_{5,1}^{2}(t)+E_{5,2}^{2}(t))\leq C\left(\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\right).

The proof is complete.
∎

Corollary 8.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and (
2.5
), according to Lemma
5.1
, Lemma
5.2
and Lemma
8.6
, there exists a positive constant
A\geq\max\{A_{3},A_{4},A_{6}\}=:A_{7},
such that if
A\geq A_{7}
, there holds

\displaystyle E_{2}(t)\leq C\left(\|(\partial_{x}^{2}n_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(u_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}+1\right)=:E_{2}.

Appendix A
Some useful estimates and lemmas in the proof

A.1.
Several useful lemmas

We first prove an embedding inequality for non-zero modulus functions.

Lemma A.1
.

Let
f
be a function such that
f_{\neq}\in H^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{3}),
there holds

\|f_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq C\|\partial_{x}f_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq C\|\nabla f_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}.

Proof.

It follows from Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality immediately and we omit it.
∎

The following lemma can be used to estimate the
L^{\infty}
norm for the zero mode.

Lemma A.2

(Lemma 3.1 in
[
8
]
)
.

For a given function
f(x,y,z)
and
f_{0}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}}{f}(t,x,y,z)dx,
we have

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}f_{0}\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|f_{0}\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

(A.1)

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left(\|\partial_{y}f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla f_{0}\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}f_{0}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}f_{0}\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\left(\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\right),

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C(\|\partial_{y}f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|f_{0}\|_{L^{2}}),

where
\alpha
is a constant with
\alpha\in\left(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4}\right).

The following lemma can be used to estimate the
L^{\infty}
norm for the non-zero mode. We only prove
\eqref{sob_result_2}_{1,2}
, and the remaining results are similar to Lemma 3.2 in
[
8
]
. The proof is omitted.

Lemma A.3
.

For a given function
g=g(x,y,z)
and
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4})
, if
g_{0}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}}{g}(t,x,y,z)dx=0,
then we have

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}\nabla g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\nabla g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big),

(A.2)

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{z}\nabla g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\nabla g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big),

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\min\big\{\|\partial_{y}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}\nabla g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|g\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\nabla g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|g\|_{L^{2}}\big\},

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,y}L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}\big),

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}g\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}\big),

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{y,z}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}\|g\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha},

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y,x}}\leq C(\|g\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{z}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}\|g\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}),

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}L^{2}_{x,z}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{y}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|g\|_{L^{2}}\big),

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,z}L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}g\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{\frac{3}{2}-2\alpha}_{L^{2}}\big).

Proof.

Due to
g_{0}=0
, we denote
g(x,y,z)
by

g=\sum_{k_{1},k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z},k_{1}\neq 0}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}(y){\rm e}^{i(k_{1}x+k_{3}z)},

then

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\leq C\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}(\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}).

(A.3)

First, there holds

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{\|k_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|k_{3}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}|k_{1}k_{3}|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}+\|k_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}|k_{1}|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{1}|^{\alpha}(1+|k_{3}|^{\alpha})}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{\|k_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|k_{1}k_{3}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|k_{3}^{2}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|k_{1}\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}|k_{1}|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}}{|k_{1}|^{\alpha}(1+|k_{3}|^{\alpha})}.

Using H
\rm\ddot{o}
lder’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\partial_{y}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}\partial_{y}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big).

(A.4)

Thus, one deduces

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{k_{1}\neq 0,k_{3}\in\mathbb{Z}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{y}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\frac{\|k_{1}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|k_{1}k_{3}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|k_{3}^{2}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}_{y}}+\|k_{1}\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}_{y}}\|\widehat{g}_{k_{1},k_{3}}\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}_{y}}}{|k_{1}|^{\alpha}(1+|k_{3}|^{\alpha})}

(A.5)

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|\partial_{x}g\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{z}g\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{2}g\|^{\alpha-\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}\big).

Combining (
A.3
), (
A.4
) and (
A.5
), we finish the proof of
\eqref{sob_result_1}_{1}.

Similarly, we can prove
\eqref{sob_result_1}_{2}.

∎

Lemma A.4
.

For a given function
g=g(x,y,z),
if
g_{0}=\frac{1}{|\mathbb{T}|}\int_{\mathbb{T}}g(t,x,y,z)dx=0,
there holds

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{y,z}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})g\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{y,x}}\leq C\|(\partial_{x},\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})g\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Let
G(X,Y,Z)
such that
G(x,V(t,y,z),z)=g(x,y,z).
Note that
(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})g(x,y,z)=\partial_{Z}G(x,V(t,y,z),z)
and
\|g\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{x}g\|_{L^{2}}.
Then the results follow from
\eqref{sob_result_2}_{3}
and
\eqref{sob_result_2}_{7}.

∎

Lemma A.5

(
Lemma 5.5
in
[
34
]
)
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
8.1
,
if
\partial_{x}f_{1}=0,P_{0}f_{2}=0,
it holds that

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f_{1}\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}(f_{1}f_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

\|\nabla(f_{1}f_{2})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f_{1}\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|f_{2}\|_{H^{1}}+\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f_{2}\|_{H^{1}}\right),

and for
j\in\{2,3\},

\|[\partial_{j}\triangle^{-1},f_{1}]\triangle f_{2}\|_{H^{1}}\leq C\|f_{1}\|_{H^{2}}\left(\|\nabla f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})\nabla f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Next, we introduce a logarithmic Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality that plays an important role in estimation
\|n_{0}\|_{L^{2}}
and can be found in
[
27
]
.

Lemma A.6
.

Let
\mathcal{M}
be a 2D Riemannian compact manifold. For all
m>0,
there exists a constant
C(m)
such that for all nonnegative functions
f\in L^{1}(\mathcal{M})
such that
f\log f\in L^{1}
, if
\int_{\mathcal{M}}fdx=m,
then

\int_{\mathcal{M}}f\log fdx+\frac{2}{m}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\int_{\mathcal{M}}f(x)f(y)\log d(x,y)\geq-C(m),

(A.6)

where
d(x,y)
is the distance on the Riemannian manifold.

The following Gagliardo-Nirenberg-Sobolev inequality on
\mathbb{T}^{n}
is frequently used in the proof.

Lemma A.7

(Lemma 9.2 in
[
22
]
)
.

Suppose
f\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{n}),n\geq 2,
and the set where
f
vanishes is nonempty. Assume that
q,r>0,\infty>q>r,
and
\frac{1}{n}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{r}>0.
Then

\|f\|_{L^{q}}\leq C(n,q)\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}}^{\theta}\|f\|_{L^{r}}^{1-\theta},\quad\theta=\frac{\frac{1}{r}-\frac{1}{q}}{\frac{1}{n}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{r}}.

For a fixed
n,
the constant
C(n,q)
is bounded uniformly when
q
varies in any compact set in
(0,\infty).

A.2.
Elliptic estimates

The following elliptic estimates are necessary.

Lemma A.8
.

Let
c_{0}
and
n_{0}
be the zero mode of
c
and
n
, respectively, satisfying

-\triangle c_{0}+\bar{n}=n_{0},

then there hold

\displaystyle\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|n_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|n_{0}(t)-\bar{n}\|_{L^{3}}

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|n_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}},

(A.7)

for any
t\geq 0
.

Proof.

The basic energy estimates yield

\displaystyle\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+|\bar{n}|^{2}|\mathbb{T}|^{2}=\|n_{0}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

which implies
\eqref{eq:elliptic}_{1}
.
Using Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and H
\ddot{\rm o}
lder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{3}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{3}}\leq C\|n_{0}(t)-\bar{n}\|_{L^{3}},

which gives
\eqref{eq:elliptic}_{2}
.

Moreover, it follows from Lemma
A.7
and
\eqref{eq:elliptic}_{1}
that

\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|\nabla c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\|\triangle c_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|n_{0}(t)\|_{L^{2}},

which gives
\eqref{eq:elliptic}_{3}.

∎

Lemma A.9
.

There holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{j}\triangle c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial_{x}^{j}n_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{j}\nabla c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{j}n_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}},

for any
t\geq 0
and
j\geq 0
.

Proof.

By integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\|\triangle c_{\neq}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}=\|n_{\neq}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and Lemma
A.1
, we obtain

\|\nabla c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\left(\|c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\|\triangle c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{7}{8}}+\|c_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\right)\leq C\|n_{\neq}(t)\|_{L^{2}}.

∎

A.3.
Space-time estimates

First, we need to prove the following space-time estimate, and this result
is also an improvement on previous time-space estimates
[
7
,
34
]
, which allows us to estimate the non-zero mode
n_{\neq}
in the periodic domain
\mathbb{T}^{3}.

Proposition A.1
.

Assume that
f
satisfies

\partial_{t}f-\frac{1}{A}\triangle f+\left(y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}\right)\partial_{x}f=\partial_{x}f_{1}+f_{2}+\nabla\cdot f_{3}

for
t\in[0,T],
where
f
,
f_{1}
,
f_{2}
and
f_{3}
are given functions and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=P_{0}f_{2}=P_{0}f_{3}=0
.
As long as

\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}<c,

(A.8)

for some small
c
independent of
A
and
t,
then there holds

\displaystyle\|f_{\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C\left(\|(f_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right),

where “
a
” is a positive constant.

Proof.

Our proof was inspired the coordinate transform method of
[
34
]
.
First, we state the following result, which is classical.

Lemma A.10
.

Let
g:\mathbb{T}^{3}\to\mathbb{R}^{3}
be a
C^{1}
map such that
\|\nabla g\|_{L^{\infty}}<\frac{1}{2}.
Then it holds that

(i)

\|f\circ({\rm{Id}}+g)\|_{L^{p}}\sim\|f\|_{L^{p}},\|\nabla\left(f\circ({\rm{Id}+g})\right)\|_{L^{p}}\sim\|\nabla f\|_{L^{p}}
for every
1\leq p\leq+\infty
and
f\in W^{1,p}.
Here
A\sim B
means
C^{-1}A\leq B\leq CA
for some absolute constant
C.

(ii)

There exists a unique
C^{1}
solution
h
to
h(x,y,z)=g\left((x,y,x)+h(x,y,z)\right)
satisfying
\|\nabla h\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\nabla g\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Then, we define the map
({\rm{Id}}+g):\mathbb{T}^{3}\to\mathbb{R}^{3}
by

\displaystyle(x,y,z)\mapsto(X,Y,Z)=\left(x,V(t,y,z),z\right),\quad V=y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}.

Denote

\displaystyle F(t,x,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle=f(t,x,y,z),

(A.9)

\displaystyle F_{j}(t,x,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle=f_{j}(t,x,y,z),~~~{\rm for}~~~j=1,2,

\displaystyle F_{3}(t,x,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle={\rm{div}}~f_{3}(t,x,y,z).

According to (
A.8
), by choosing
c
small enough, there holds

\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{A}\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}<\frac{1}{2},

which along with Lemma
A.10
implies that
F(t,X,Y,Z)\in C^{1}
is well-defined.

Let
w_{t}(t,Y,Z),w_{y}(t,Y,Z)
and
w_{z}(t,Y,Z)
be such that

\displaystyle w_{t}(t,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}=\partial_{t}V(t,y,z),

(A.10)

\displaystyle w_{y}(t,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}=\partial_{y}V(t,y,z)-1,

\displaystyle w_{z}(t,V(t,y,z),z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}=\partial_{z}V(t,y,z).

Direct calculations indicate that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{t}+w_{t}\partial_{Y}\right)F,\quad\partial_{x}f=\partial_{X}F,

(A.11)

\displaystyle\partial_{y}f

\displaystyle=\left(1+w_{y}\right)\partial_{Y}F,\quad\partial_{z}f=\left(\partial_{Z}+w_{z}\partial_{Y}\right)F.

Therefore, we obtain that

V\partial_{x}f=Y\partial_{X}F,

and

\displaystyle\triangle f=

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{y}^{2}+\partial_{z}^{2}\right)f

(A.12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{X}^{2}F+\partial_{y}\left[\left(1+w_{y}\right)\partial_{Y}F\right]+\partial_{z}\left[\left(\partial_{Z}+w_{z}\partial_{Y}\right)F\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{X}^{2}+\partial_{Y}^{2}+\partial_{Z}^{2}\right)F+\left[\left(1+w_{y}\right)^{2}+w_{z}^{2}-1\right]\partial_{Y}^{2}F+2w_{z}\partial_{Z}\partial_{Y}F+\left(w_{yy}+w_{zz}\right)\partial_{Y}F

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\triangle F+G\partial_{Y}^{2}F+2w_{z}\partial_{Z}\partial_{Y}F+H\partial_{Y}F,

where

\displaystyle G=\left(1+w_{y}\right)^{2}+w_{z}^{2}-1,~~~H(t,V(t,y,z),z)=\frac{\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}.

Using (
A.11
)-(
A.12
), we write the operator
\mathcal{L}_{V}
into

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}f=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f-\frac{1}{A}\triangle f+V\partial_{x}f

(A.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{t}+w_{t}\partial_{Y}\right)F-\frac{1}{A}\left(\triangle F+G\partial_{Y}^{2}F+2w_{z}\partial_{Z}\partial_{Y}F+H\partial_{Y}F\right)+Y\partial_{X}F

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}F-\frac{1}{A}\left(G\partial_{Y}^{2}F+2w_{z}\partial_{Z}\partial_{Y}F\right)+\left(w_{t}-\frac{H}{A}\right)\partial_{Y}F,

where
\mathcal{L}=\partial_{t}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle+Y\partial_{X}.

Due to (
A.10
) and (
A.11
), there hold

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}F=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{y}f}{\partial_{y}V},\quad\partial_{Z}F=\partial_{z}f-\frac{\partial_{z}V\partial_{y}f}{\partial_{y}V},

\displaystyle G(t,V(t,y,z),z)=

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)^{2}-1

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\partial_{y}V)^{2}+(\partial_{z}V)^{2}-1,

which imply that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}G+2\partial_{Z}w_{z}=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{y}\left((\partial_{y}V)^{2}+(\partial_{z}V)^{2}-1\right)}{\partial_{y}V}+2\left[\partial_{z}-\left(\frac{\partial_{z}V}{\partial_{y}V}\right)\partial_{y}\right]\partial_{z}V

(A.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\partial_{y}^{2}V+\frac{2\partial_{z}V\partial_{y}\partial_{z}V}{\partial_{y}V}+2\partial_{z}^{2}V-2\frac{\partial_{z}V}{\partial_{y}V}\partial_{y}\partial_{z}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\triangle V=\frac{2}{A}\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}=2H.

Combining (
A.13
) and (
A.14
), one deduces that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{V}f=\mathcal{L}F-\frac{1}{A}\partial_{Y}(G\partial_{Y}F)-\frac{2}{A}\partial_{Z}\left(w_{z}\partial_{Y}F\right)+\left(w_{t}+\frac{H}{A}\right)\partial_{Y}F.

(A.15)

From (
A.9
), we get
\mathcal{L}_{V}f=\partial_{X}F_{1}+F_{2}+F_{3}.
Then (
A.15
) yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}F=

\displaystyle\partial_{X}F_{1}+F_{2}+F_{3}+\frac{1}{A}\partial_{Y}(G\partial_{Y}F)+\frac{2}{A}\partial_{Z}\left(w_{z}\partial_{Y}F\right)-\left(w_{t}+\frac{H}{A}\right)\partial_{Y}F.

This implies that

\displaystyle\|F\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\bigg(\|F(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla F_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}F_{3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(A.16)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\left\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\left(G,2w_{z}\right)\partial_{Y}F\right\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\left\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\left(w_{t}+\frac{H}{A}\right)\partial_{Y}F\right\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle C\Big(\|F(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla F_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}F_{3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+K_{1}+K_{2}\Big).

For
K_{1},
recall that
w_{z}=\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}
and

G=(\partial_{y}V)^{2}+(\partial_{z}V)^{2}-1=\left(\frac{\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}+1\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{z}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}\right)^{2}-1=\frac{|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}|^{2}}{A^{2}}+\frac{2\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}.

Then using (
A.8
), one deduces

\displaystyle K_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|G\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left\|2w_{z}\right\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\big)\frac{\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{Y}F\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}}{A}

(A.17)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(c^{4}+c^{2})\|F\|_{X_{a}}^{2},

where we use the Poincar
\acute{\rm e}
inequality
\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

For
K_{2},
as
w_{t}=\frac{\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A}
and
H=\frac{\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}}{A},
using (
A.8
), we have

\displaystyle K_{2}\leq

\displaystyle A^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\left\|w_{t}\right\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left\|\frac{H}{A}\right\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{Y}F\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{Cc^{2}}{A^{\frac{2}{3}}}\|F\|_{X_{a}}^{2}.

(A.18)

Combining (
A.17
) with (
A.18
), then choosing
c
small enough, we get by (
A.16
) that

\displaystyle\|F\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\bigg(\|F(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla F_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(A.19)

\displaystyle+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}F_{3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\bigg).

It follows from Lemma
A.10
that

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla F\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},~~~\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

(A.20)

and

\displaystyle\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla F_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2},~~~\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}F_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

(A.21)

Next we claim

\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}F\|_{L^{2}}\sim\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}f\|_{L^{2}}.

(A.22)

As
V=y+\frac{\mathbf{U}_{2}(t,y,z)}{A}
and (
A.8
), we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla^{2}V\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1+\frac{\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{A}+\frac{\|\nabla^{2}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{A}\leq 1+C\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{H^{2}}}{A}\leq C.

(A.23)

Denote

\displaystyle\check{F}_{1}=\triangle^{-1}F,\quad\check{f}_{1}(t,x,y,z)=\check{F}_{1}(t,x,V(t,y,z),z),

\displaystyle\check{f}_{2}=\triangle^{-1}f,\quad\check{f}_{2}(t,x,y,z)=\check{F}_{2}(t,x,V(t,y,z),z).

Then using (
A.20
) and (
A.23
), one deduces

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}F\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=-<F,\check{F}_{1}>=-<f,\partial_{y}V\check{f}_{1}>

(A.24)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle<\nabla\check{f}_{2},\nabla(\partial_{y}V\check{f}_{1})>\leq\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla(\partial_{y}V\check{f}_{1})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\nabla V\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\check{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}V\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\check{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\check{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\check{f}_{1}\|_{L^{2}},

which implies

\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

(A.25)

On the other hand, there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=-<f,\check{f}_{2}>=-<F,\check{F}_{2}/\partial_{y}V>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle<\nabla\check{F}_{1},\nabla(\check{F}_{2}/\partial_{y}V)>\leq\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla(\check{F}_{2}/\partial_{y}V)\|_{L^{2}}.

Notice that
\partial_{y}V=\frac{1}{A}\partial_{y}\mathbf{U}_{2}+1
and
\frac{1}{A}\|\nabla\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}<\frac{1}{2},
we have
\frac{1}{2}<\partial_{y}V<\frac{3}{2}.

Along this with (
A.23
), we get

\|\nabla(1/\partial_{y}V)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C,
and

\displaystyle\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}f\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\nabla\check{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\check{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

(A.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\check{F}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}},

which gives

\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}.

(A.27)

Combining with (
A.24
)-(
A.27
), we conclude that

\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}F\|_{L^{2}}=\|\nabla\check{F}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\sim\|\nabla\check{f}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}=\|\nabla\triangle^{-1}f\|_{L^{2}},

which indicates (
A.22
) holds. Similarly, we obtain

\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla\triangle^{-1}F_{3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}f_{3}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

(A.28)

From (
A.19
), (
A.21
) and (
A.28
), we complete the proof.
∎

To estimate the coupled terms
(\triangle u_{2,\neq},\partial_{x}\omega_{2,\neq}),
we also need the following proposition.

Proposition A.2
.

Let
(h_{1},h_{2})
satisfy

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\mathcal{L}_{V}h_{1}=\nabla\cdot g_{1},\\
\mathcal{L}_{V}h_{2}+\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}h_{1}=\nabla\cdot g_{2},\end{array}\right.

for
t\in[0,T].
Assume that

\frac{\|\triangle\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{2}}}{A}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq c

(A.29)

for some small constant
c
independent of
A
and
T.
If

P_{0}h_{1}=P_{0}h_{2}=P_{0}g_{1}=P_{0}g_{2}=0,
then for
a\geq 0,
it holds that

\|h_{1,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|h_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C\left(\|(h_{1,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(h_{2,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Proof.

From Proposition A.2 in
[
8
]
, we find that if

(h_{1},h_{2})
satisfy

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\partial_{t}h_{1}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle h_{1}+y\partial_{x}h_{1}=\nabla\cdot g_{1},\\
\\
\partial_{t}h_{2}-\frac{1}{A}\triangle h_{2}+y\partial_{x}h_{2}+\partial_{x}\partial_{z}\triangle^{-1}h_{2}=\nabla\cdot g_{2},\end{array}\right.

for
t\in[0,T],
then it holds that

\|h_{1,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}+\|h_{2,\neq}\|^{2}_{X_{a}}\leq C\left(\|(h_{1,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|(h_{2,\rm in})_{\neq}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}g_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Next following the same route as in Proposition
A.1
, we complete the proof.
∎

Proposition A.3

(
Proposition 4.7
in
[
34
]
)
.

Let
f
satisfy

\mathcal{L}_{V}f=f_{1}+f_{2}+f_{3}

for
t\in[0,T].
Moreover,
\mathbf{U}_{2}
satisfies (
A.29
)
and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=P_{0}f_{2}=P_{0}f_{3}=0,
then for
a\geq 0,
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{2}f_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|(f_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\triangle f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+A^{\frac{1}{3}}\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}-\kappa\partial_{y})f_{2,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\partial_{x}f_{3,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\big).

Proposition A.4

(
Proposition 4.9
in
[
34
]
)
.

Let
f
satisfy
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}_{V}}f=f_{1}
for
t\in[0,T].
Moreover,
\mathbf{U}_{2}
satisfies (
A.29
)
and
P_{0}f=P_{0}f_{1}=0,
then for
a\geq 0,
it holds that

\|\triangle f_{\neq}\|_{X_{a}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|(\triangle f_{\rm in})_{\neq}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+A\|{\rm e}^{aA^{-\frac{1}{3}}t}\nabla f_{1,\neq}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right).
